# EFMI Vulkan - Current development state

> Shortened 2026-11-10 (docs/architecture-review-2.1.md, P4-1): the rounds from 2.1.0 on are here; everything before is `.ai-memory/archive/CURRENT_STATE-until-2.0.9.md`. The architecture as it is now: ARCHITECTURE.md.

> Branches: main and engine-work, the branch of the worktree `.worktrees/fix` where the work is done; main is fast-forwarded to verified commits. Local commits and the remote-tracking ref are distinguished below.


## Active priority: original configuration execution rewrite (2026-11-16)

- The user explicitly rejects character/Mod package loading in the compatibility route. Remove that architecture completely, follow the original loader and lazy resource execution, then finish the Vulkan-native route. This supersedes package discovery/activation choices; see docs/configuration-execution-rewrite.md.

## D-306: Generic vertex layouts use exact shader semantics (2026-11-16)

- Source/four development DLLs are 3.2.4 / ABI 54ef0e38e8e5228f. A configuration owns the optional exact input index, loaded from stable settings/shader-inputs.bin or EFMI_VK_SHADER_INPUTS. Bounded immutable shader lookups feed the original VertexProfile evaluator. File stride changes need no semantic names; named edits require exact module identity. No character/profile chooses a load or draw.
- Each ordered explicit, caller or implicit draw preflights its effective declaration and bounds, requests an asynchronous Vulkan variant, retains it with the recording and restores the selected pipeline afterward. Selected native shader fixes/hunting stages remain intact. Dynamic rendering and compatible private classic output passes accept supported static declaration changes; dynamic vertex input/stride, numeric-class conversion, unknown semantics and declaration-order-dependent edits retain explicit refusals.
- configuration-layout-before.out reproduces stride/offset failures (6/12); the initial scope test passes 15/18 with dynamic rendering still refused. Final configuration-layout-regression.out passes geometry 122, tracker 825, native calls 60, module boundary 7 and text. Its old dynamic-recreation refusal assertion leaks a fake test handle when recreation now succeeds, causing recipe 397/407 and wrapper 62/63. Updating that assertion, releasing its handle and adding dynamic-input/stride guards gives recipe 410, cache 81, snapshots 52 and wrapper 63/63 in configuration-layout-variants-final.out. The expanded final ABI check includes private/shared geometry headers.
- configuration-layout-runtime.out freshly rebuilds and links the original interpreter test with current objects and passes 553. D-305's direct runtime invocation reused the earlier linked executable; its 553 result alone did not verify that round's frontend changes. This fresh check covers both D-305 and D-306. Development builds, actual sandbox pixel results and historical candidate/game results remain distinct.
- Original CommandList.cpp 282-316 applies ordered semantic edits; HackerContext.cpp 836-859 temporarily binds/restores the layout. DX11 supplies reference-counted lifetime with deferred destruction, hazard tracking and one-context order. Vulkan adds explicit immutable variant ownership and restores only temporary pipeline state, preserving persistent IA resources.
- Continue application IA/resource facts, retained original Core imports and ordered GPU insertion; migrate legacy acceptance fixtures, then native queue/access/readiness and capture work. The original Core is not yet executable on live game resources. No new candidate, game load/deployment or installed/system mutation; candidate 3.2.1/root dist 2.1.8/game 2.1.7 remain historical.

## D-305: Generic file geometry and persistent IA state (2026-11-16)

- Source/four development DLLs are 3.2.3 / ABI 54ef0e38e8e5228f. Original root command lists now emit ordered vertex/index assignments. File declarations use the shared lazy upload cache, with immutable byte extents and completed publication waits. No character/package selects geometry.
- IA state persists across calls until the application rebinds the named slots. Immutable command snapshots and bounded, deduplicated owners retain all used uploads through completion and module exchange. Later calls validate persistent geometry even when their matching lists are empty or refused. Dispatch ignores existing IA state; compute lists cannot assign IA bindings.
- Original CommandList.cpp 10064-10072 calls IASetVertexBuffers/IASetIndexBuffer. DX11 supplies reference-counted lifetime with deferred destruction, hazard tracking and one-context order. Vulkan now supplies explicit retained state, bounds validation and publication dependencies for this immutable-file subset.
- configuration-geometry-before.out reproduces the former descriptor-address refusal (3/6). Final configuration-geometry-regression.out passes geometry 52, native calls 60 and native files 42. Geometry covers actual pixels, 16/32-bit indices, bounds refusal, post commands/restoration, two queues, F10 file refresh and module exchange, with clean core/synchronization validation. Its tracker step fails one stale named-shader-only activation assumption (812/813); the updated root-program assertion and 12 new lifetime/persistence cases pass 825/825 in configuration-geometry-tracker.out. Original INI runtime passes 553 in configuration-geometry-runtime.out.
- Geometry still requires a supported static declaration and matching file stride; semantic/layout variants, owned GPU geometry, application-index range proof and retained cross-draw application IA references remain open. Original Core application imports and in-draw GPU insertion are not implemented by this change. Continue generic semantics/layouts and those adapters, then native I-01/I-04/I-05. No compatibility/game completion is claimed.
- No candidate refresh or game/installed-file/system mutation. Candidate 3.2.1, root dist 2.1.8 and last real-game 2.1.7 are historical, distinct artifacts.

## D-301: Detached color targets can become sampled inputs (2026-11-16)

- Runtime 3.2.1 / ABI 7c4613bb6a168795 fixes the supported subset of BUG-157 / I-04. A non-original retained color target initially in COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL can be written, detached and sampled through an exact-view/subresource proof. Actual output splits establish GENERAL for its read phases; reattachment uses the tracked current layout, and scope end restores the original layout even for an inactive target.
- Original CommandList.cpp 10043-10051 binds shader resources; 10094-10119 retains other OM views, changes an output and releases temporary references. DX11 supplies reference-counted lifetime with deferred destruction, hazard tracking and one-context order. Vulkan now records those proven phase transitions explicitly rather than requiring this input to start in a readable layout.
- The 3.2.0 baseline d301-before.out passes 9/12: the independent write control succeeds but the ordered read is refused with Native image layout transition must be prepared before the render pass. d301-focused.out passes 37/37 after the fix, including classic, dynamic rendering, RenderPass2, repeated phases and actual module exchange, with independent pixels and layout-restoration validation.
- d301-build.out builds four matching DLLs and passes package 148 / tracker 838. Nine new deterministic cases cover detachment proof, repeated phases, earlier/unexecuted splits, overlap, recycled identities and wider ranges. d301-sandbox-build.out adds actual owned dynamic-rendering fixtures and an application-owned COLOR layout/readback oracle.
- d301-regression.out passes every native-image-phases and module-boundary step: image phases 125, character replacement 377, matched descriptors 72, output memory 52, mixed images 35, output transactions 64, uniform persistence 52, application images 79 and Vulkan validation 76, plus all five module scope checks and the static boundary check. Text and diff checks pass. Simultaneous accesses, allocation aliases, queue and synchronization2 guards retain their checks.
- The restoration flag is shared scope state, so this revision needs matching host/backend binaries; a backend-only update to a 3.2.0 host is not supported. The separately verified candidate stays 3.2.0 until refreshed. I-04 still needs other phase transitions, original-output aliases and automatic conflicting-input nulling. I-01 production resource/queue scheduling, I-05 one-shot compute and broader I-06 inputs remain open.
- No game load/deployment, launcher/system mutation or remote write. Root dist is unchanged. The earlier temporary-directory cleanup rejection is not retried or bypassed.

## D-298: Independent bone masks for exact semantic layouts (2026-11-16)

- Runtime 3.1.9 / ABI 347c72b0cc7d0e40 fixes BUG-155: one immutable blend stream can supply multiple exact-semantic declarations. Each generation caches the validated mask by stream, index/weight offsets and widths; each draw list retains its own mask and layout identity. The default stream metadata is never overwritten by these interpretations. The cache is bounded to 4,096 layouts.
- Original CommandList.cpp 178-316 applies ordered element edits per draw; HackerContext.cpp restores/releases the input layout after drawing. DX11 supplies reference-counted lifetime with deferred destruction, hazard tracking and one-context order. This Vulkan change separates input interpretation from immutable buffer identity; existing resource owners, ordering guards and shader-input proof remain necessary.
- d298-before.out reproduces the refusal at 9/10 with the old different-semantic-layout diagnostic. Final build passes package 148 and tracker 829; all four DLLs are 3.1.9. The new GPU semantics suite passes 102/102, including three layouts of one stream, moving bones and actual module exchange. An initial exchange fixture returned before exchanging; correcting the fixture produces 11/11 and checks the resulting generation.
- d298-regression.out input-semantics passes reader/frontend/INI (543), own-mesh capture (63), cold packages (51), readiness (20), tracker/package and Vulkan validation (76). Its merged-opacity step initially passes 41/48: cold asynchronous variants execute 36-38 times within 30 frames, below the existing >40 oracle; all pixel/depth and validation checks pass. Keeping every oracle and increasing only merged replay to 60 frames gives 48/48 in d298-opacity-cold.out (110 executions per case). The failed aggregate is recorded separately from the passing targeted rerun. Module boundary passes 7/7.
- Next: remove the analogous same-stream width restriction from the existing limited blend contract when no semantic cache is available. Unknown arbitrary semantics, numeric-class conversion, inherited application inputs, declaration-order edits, I-01/I-04 and in-draw CustomShader insertion remain open. The separately verified candidate stays at 3.1.8. No game load/deployment, launcher/system mutation or remote write; root dist is unchanged.

- Retention follow-up: automatic approval review rejected deletion of this round's six temporary build subdirectories (tests, native-merged-opacity, native-own-capture, replay, shader-input-test, xxmi-host), returning only blocked by policy. The command did not execute; these artifacts remain. No deletion bypass was attempted.

## D-286: Actual queue overlap and rejected serialization shortcut (2026-11-15; source remains 3.1.3)

- I-01 now has actual pending GPU work rather than alternating drained frames. The new headless queue_overlap fixture submits a timeline-waiting consumer before its producer, verifies the consumer fence is pending, then checks all 64 copied words. No QueueWaitIdle/DeviceWaitIdle is used. A third private queue is used only for the scheduler experiment.
- Original HackerContext.cpp 1079 runs post lists and restores/releases shaders on one DX11 context. DX11 supplies reference-counted lifetime with deferred destruction, hazard tracking and one-context order. Vulkan owners and per-queue completion counters do not supply the missing cross-queue access order.
- A global completion chain demonstrably stalls this otherwise valid graph. Both counters stay zero and a bounded fence wait times out. Host signal 1, below pending device signal 2, legally releases the experiment; the consumer reads initial data and all work exits normally. Do not implement this rejected shortcut in the loader.
- Readiness markers plus per-queue gates select the later ready producer before the earlier waiting consumer. A private GPU release queue waits on readiness and previous completion, then signals the selected gate. This yields correct data. CPU counter observations select work; GPU edges carry the memory dependency. An additional 03238 validation canary rejects a binary consumer whose producer's gate signal has not been submitted; forwarding succeeds once the complete dependency chain is submitted.
- d286-build-binary.out builds the standalone fixture without warnings; production source and all four DLL stamps remain 3.1.3 / 134dd78b13c38aaa. d286-binary.out passes 16/16 native checks per entry (none/layer/inject), 18/18 wrapper checks and text 688. I-01 remains open until these contracts and introduced graphics accesses are implemented together. Continue I-06's actual application-input and vertex-layout adapters without substituting sandbox evidence for real-game verification. No game, deployment, launcher, system or remote action.

## Architecture review for Endfield dual-route scope (2026-11-15; `issues.md`)

- The user clarified that the loader has two routes—Route 1 (DX11 compatibility for EFMI Mods) and Route 2 (Vulkan 3DMigoto)—and that **both routes target only *Arknights: Endfield*** (`Endfield.exe`), not arbitrary Vulkan applications.
- Wrote `issues.md` at the repository root documenting nine architectural issues (`I-01` through `I-09`) against source `3.0.4` (`D-276`):
  - `I-01` (Critical, Route 2): `native_targets.cpp:256`, `native_descriptors.cpp:171`, `native_images.cpp:176`, and `gpu_analysis.cpp:105` hard-refuse when `device->application_queue_count != 1 || device->material_queues.size() != 1`. Because *Endfield* creates two graphics queues (`BUG-008`, `BUG-050`, `BUG-052`), Route 2 output switches (`o0-o7`/`oD`), application resource bindings, image layout transitions, and `F8` GPU frame analysis unconditionally refuse in the real game.
  - `I-02` (High, Route 2): Scope drift into Vulkan features unused by *Endfield*'s Unity `2021.3.34f5` renderer (Dynamic Rendering `vkCmdBeginRendering`, `VK_KHR_imageless_framebuffer`, `GS`/`HS`/`DS`, push descriptors, `VK_EXT_vertex_input_dynamic_state`).
  - `I-03` (High, Both): Four split draw executors (`record`, `record_own_mesh`, `record_textures`, `native_calls`) duplicate target/shadow/capture/restore state and omit `prepare_native_descriptors` (`vk_binding`) from matched mesh/texture replacement draws.
  - `I-04` (Medium, Route 2): Combinatorial pair whitelisting (`independent_descriptor`) in `native_targets.cpp` instead of unified pass-boundary barrier and descriptor planning.
  - `I-05` (High, Both, `L-04` / `ISSUE-012`): First-use `cache->await` and `textures->bind` waits held under `device->res_mx` shared lock and `mod_mx`.
  - `I-06` (Medium, Route 1, `P1-5` / `LIMIT-035`): In-draw `CustomShader` reading game `vs-t0`/`vs-cbN` bindings, cross-slot application texture rebinding, and general `InputLayout` edits.
  - `I-07` (Medium, Both): `EFVK_ABI` coupling from exposing `native_targets.h`, `native_images.h`, and `pipeline_variant.h` in `character_module.h`.
  - `I-08` (Low, Both, `L-09`): Legacy `M2Backend` and `.bin` round-trip validation in `load_mod`.
  - `I-09` (High, Both): 86-version verification gap between `dist/` (`2.1.8`) and `HEAD` (`3.0.4`).
- No source or binary changes; `scripts/check_text.py` passes (`674` files clean).

## Active source 2.3.6: Retained application sampled images, D-206 (2026-11-14; sandbox)

- D-205 is merged as 04cc6f8. Native application sampled images now use current recording-local layouts, retained original descriptor fallbacks and per-call refresh. Safe read layouts bind directly; known transfer/color layouts can be temporarily sampled outside render passes and restored after compute or the graphics pass. Bound descriptor sets are cloned, never updated in place. Requests survive ordinary/pushed native clones; application rebinding supersedes them.

Graphics attachment overlap/unknown scope, unknown local layout, mutable/writable image state and unsupported consumer shapes refuse or restore the original value. ## 2.2.6: Explicit native resource aliases, D-196 (2026-11-13; sandbox verified, not deployed)

- Continued after the user corrected the premature stop at D-195. `vk_binding` metadata names a logical slot and Vulkan set/binding/array element under an original SPIR-V shader hash. All six stages query current descriptors through the original getters and matcher. Ordinary/template/pushed sets, dynamic offsets, resource/view formats, slot/declaration references, fuzzy matches and F10 are covered; conflicting metadata isolates its INI without losing unrelated inputs. No per-character tables.
- Original ParseTargetPipelineSlot and ResourceCopyTarget getters supply semantics. Buffer size is the visible range; image buffer properties return -3. View Format and resource match_format are distinct. Slot BindFlags translates creation usage; a custom declaration's BindFlags comes from its own declaration even when referencing the application. Read-only tracked image uploads may have hashes; writable resources have metadata only. Queries add no GPU access or ownership. DX11's reference-counted deferred destruction, hazard tracking and context order remain explicit requirements for the native executor that follows.
- Built and checked worktree host/inject/backend 2.2.6, shared ABI remains 0x3cab9df2f4f8b9fa (frontend query callback is inside the module, not an ABI boundary). Eleven selected quick/native-slots/character steps pass: units 117, source evaluation 65, both original judges, tracker 185, character package/replacement 115/377, live INI 221, native resources 69 and validation 76, plus text/matrix. Final pair is rebuilt in build/d196-declaration-build.out. The final resource step passes all existing 69 checks; the additional declaration BindFlags case passes 4/4 in d196-reference-flags-final.out (73 checks across these final runs). Its first expectation incorrectly treated override_bind_flags as accumulated bind_flags; GetResourceBindFlags reads the latter, zero before this unused declaration is substantiated. No full suite/corpus/installed-Mod sweep.
- Intermediate build/test problems were identified: stale objects after editing during a build, an incorrect descriptor-mode fixture argument, and relinking a character test while it was running (separate d196-character-build.out succeeds). The final pair is d196-declaration-build.out; d196-final-properties.out and d196-reference-flags-final.out cover its property behavior. Root dist stays 2.1.8, latest recorded game evidence stays 2.1.7. No deployment, game load/attachment/module exchange, launcher/system change or remote operation.

## 2.1.4: notices on every presented image (BUG-092, 2026-11-11)

- The user, in the 2.1.3 session: while refreshing, the error texts flicker all the time. The status had the cause: `notices.busy` 1290 beside `drawn` 4211, a presented image without the notices whenever the overlay's three slots were all pending, which a frame generator's four presentations per rendered frame bring about.
- From the original: `Overlay::DrawOverlay` in the Present hook draws the notices with the immediate context at every Present; D3D11 orders the draws behind the frame and keeps what they use. The layer's own work per presentation needs objects of its own, and as many of them as presentations are pending: a slot is made when none is free (up to 32), and the text images are released when no notice is shown (`layer/src/notices.cpp`, docs/ini-semantics.md).

## Phase 1 of the review: a replaced draw's lists against a draw state (2026-11-11, D-161 to D-163, docs/ini-semantics.md)

- A session of its own in a worktree (branch `claude/phase1-draw-state-ab9740`), merged into main after the whole suite. Sandbox only: nothing deployed, nothing loaded into the game.
- From the original: ResourceCopyOperation::run, CopyResourceToResource, ResourceCopyTarget::GetResource and SetResource (every source and destination is a call on D3D11's context or a custom resource; nothing refuses), CommandListOperand::evaluate (every operand has a value), `_RunCommandList`, SkipCommand. What D3D11's runtime does for it: a context that holds any binding at any slot, objects a custom resource can keep, one context's order.
- P1-1, D-161: `frontend/xxmi/draw_state.h` (`DrawState`, `SlotValue`); the adapter's six tables are that one structure; every resource assignment is `Runtime::assign` and refuses nothing.
- P1-2: a draw is recorded with the state it sees (`Runtime::issue`); `honour_table()` decides per slot (honoured, ignored, degraded with a notice, that draw left out, the draw left to the game). The host tells the pipeline's stages, vertex slots (`RuntimeDraw::vertex_slots`, new) and targets.
- P1-3, D-162: the lists run to their end; `refuse` is a command that does nothing with a notice, `refuse_draw` what the host cannot do (the draw stays the game's after the lists ran). `handling = skip` after the game's draw does nothing, as in the original.
- P1-4, D-163: `operand_table()`; both tables generated into `docs/ini-execution-matrix.json` (55 and 11 rows) and checked against the source.
- P1-5 in part (`layer/src/character_program.cpp`, `character_package.*`): a vertex slot the pipeline does not read is ignored; the blend-index edit is optional (`CharacterDraw::pipeline_blend`, 8-bit bone indices). Open: TASKS.
- `refuse(` in `frontend/xxmi/draw_plan.cpp` (lines, `grep -c`): 138 on 144abc0, each able to refuse a whole draw; now 101, each a command that does nothing with a notice, and 7 lines of `refuse_draw(` (6 sites and the definition).
- Verified (sandbox, CPU only): the host probe (plan_test 67, resource planner 31, Core pools 23, native bridge 16, texture queries 26 checks), the INI runtime test (502 checks), the INI execution matrix (0 failures), the judge of replaced draws (14 cases as the original's), the sweep (58 lines: 49 as the original's, 6 degraded with a notice, 2 differ in one measure, 1 not judged; none refuses). Failing first: the build before (build/baseline-144abc0/runtime_test.exe) with the new records fails 4 cases and 23 sweep lines.
- The layer side: the layer and sandbox build with no new warning, the shared header stamp unchanged (0x4437a4d9119220e0). Unit checks for the optional blend-index edit (character package 114 checks: the pipeline's own 8-bit element kept, 8-bit bone indices adopted, one outside the skeleton refused).
- Tests of the owned runtime that stated the old rule state the new one (resource_gpu 11288 checks, validation clean): a draw whose list has a command that cannot act is planned, the notice carries the reason, and the owned scope's recorded work is kept or dropped as in a Present.
- Plan digests (scripts/check_mod_states.py; 336 key actions, 6578 states, 19 Mods, 18 drawing in the default state, 7 before). The user changed the Mods folder on 2026-11-11 13:42 to 13:55 (EFMITTL 0.5.5, Zhuangfangyi), so every digest was taken as new for changed files. The reasons were confirmed another way: the build before (a worktree at 144abc0 with the outcome dump) and this build on the same folder, per section and outcome (`EFMI_VK_PLAN_OUTCOMES`, `check_mod_states.py --explain`, new): in all 19 Mods the changed sections went from refused to planned, 173 outcomes, for two reasons only, both the offline stand-in's ("The replaced draw's render targets are not known" 147, asked by the new EFMITTL's `o0 !== null && o2 === null`; "Original buffer window unavailable" 26); and in two Mods (snowshine, akekuri) four sections each gained an empty plan in 120 states: `$active0 = 1` is now reached because the lists run to their end, the Mod's toggle keys act, and the toggled part is off there (R-02). No section went from planned to refused, and no planned section's draws changed otherwise.
- The whole suite on the final code (sandbox/run_all.py --no-build): 54 steps passed, 1 failed, "captured frame with a library call" (BUG-088, the other session's), as before the change. The six replays of single Mods use the project's copies (build/replay-mods, taken from the main worktree's build folder): the user has removed those Mods from the installation.

- A session of its own, as the round before left it: the split between two whole suite runs with nothing else changing. Sandbox only: nothing deployed, nothing loaded into the game, dist/ of the main checkout untouched.
- Before: `layer/src/character_backend.cpp`, one translation unit of 4326 lines (272076 bytes). After, eleven translation units and one internal header, 4608 lines (281442 bytes) together:

| File | Lines | Bytes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `character_backend_internal.h` (shared types and declarations) | 724 | 45262 |
| `character_backend.cpp` (the backend of a device, the module's table) | 543 | 31183 |
| `character_backend_index.cpp` (index of sections, index region hash, `character_record`) | 193 | 11097 |
| `character_backend_twins.cpp` (D-133) | 128 | 6964 |
| `character_backend_generation.cpp` (a package's life, its own Present) | 522 | 29170 |

The stage timers show nothing either (identify 0.61 and 0.60, plan 9.9 and 10.0, materials 0.63 and 0.60).

## Architecture review (2026-11-10, docs/architecture-review-2.1.md)

- The user asked for a review of the whole project, the INI interpreter above all, and a plan. Review of c69c1b6 by reading; nothing built, deployed or loaded into the game; one check run. No code changed.
- Verdict: the hosted original parser and evaluator are right and are not where Mods are lost. Mods are lost in the three backends the interpreter runs against: the plan path of replaced draws is a whitelist run all or nothing (R-01), a Mod is a character Mod by a section name and by the exporter's shape (R-03, R-04), the native Core is bound to the Core's names and to version 1.48 while the installed Core is 1.49 (R-06, R-07), one failing input can still take the folder (R-10), what is left out at start has no notice (R-11), and one failed owned command ends the folder's owned GPU work until F10 (R-12). Layer host: a failed activation leaves a character invisible (L-01), F10 does nothing after a folder refused at start (L-02), 64 packages (L-03).
- Verification: the judge never draws, so replaced draws have no comparison with the original (V-01); the installed-Mod checks assert that a plan exists, not what it is (V-02).
- Run (sandbox, CPU only): `scripts/check_ini_execution_matrix.py` on the build of 2026-10-30: 32 classes, 41 probed uses, 39 failures. `docs/ini-execution-matrix.json` still expects the refusals D-142 to D-155 lifted; the step is in the `ini` group and has been red since 2026-10-28 (BUG-079).
- Plan in the document's section 8, waiting for the user's choice: P3-0 (matrix and judge groups) and Phase 0 (containment, P0-1 to P0-9) first, then the matched-draw judge (P3-1), then the draw state in place of the whitelist (Phase 1) and Mods by what they do (Phase 2).
- Not verified: everything marked "read" or "reported" there was not reproduced by a test.

## The review's plan, the parts without the game (2026-11-10, docs/architecture-review-2.1.md section 8)

- The user, 2026-11-10: push, then do the parts that need no game session. Everything below is built and run in the sandbox only: nothing deployed, nothing loaded into the game, dist/ of the main checkout untouched. Work on branch engine-work (worktree `.worktrees/fix`).
- P3-0: `docs/ini-execution-matrix.json` states the current behaviour (decision `degraded`, with its warning required); `scripts/check_ini_execution_matrix.py` 0 failures (39 before); the judge in the `ini` and `owned` groups; a `quick` group in `sandbox/run_all.py`.
- Phase 0, frontend (D-156, a failure stays with its unit):
  - BUG-084: an unassigned LOD remap is no remap, as the original Core's `z0 = @cs-t1 != 0` (the installed Mod lastrite was left out whole).
  - P0-2, BUG-083: RegisterMod compares with the installed Core's `$version` read from main.ini (1.49), not a constant.
  - P0-3: the Core's names are checked at load and named when missing.
  - P0-4: a command that cannot act is skipped at every list depth of a tolerant root (BridgeCommand::run), with one notice through the LayerGap channel (`frontend/xxmi/layer_gap.h`, IniRuntime::gap_count / gaps_since).
  - P0-5: `prepare` names every Mod it cannot prepare; a second INI under one namespace is left out and told; an input the folder does not load with is found by halving (FolderSource::left_inputs).
  - P0-6: a CustomShader section without the owned runtime does nothing instead of refusing the load.
  - BUG-082: the owned runtime's failure is its scope's (ResourceCommands::recorded, GpuResourceRuntime::failed, a command disabled only when it recorded work); the next Present starts clean.
- Phase 0, layer host (D-158):
  - P0-1: what is left out at the first load is a notice on the screen as after F10 (`announce()`), runtime gaps are shown (three, then one "more" line), and an INI whose mesh sections match no draw is listed (`unmatched`) and said.
  - P0-7, BUG-080: a failed activation leaves the draws to the game, is counted (`activation_failed`) and retried.
  - P0-8, BUG-081: F10 reloads when no generation is loaded.
  - P0-9: the package bound is 4096 (64 before).
- P3-1 (D-157): the original as the judge of replaced draws. `sandbox/dx11_judge_scene.h` draws two game meshes under the pinned XXMI DLL (region hashes, ShaderRegex and `CheckTextureOverride = ib` as the Core's), `runtime_test.exe --judge-scene` plans the same lists, `scripts/check_dx11_judge_draws.py` compares points, primitives and variables: 10 cases agree, 2 known differences kept as such.
- P3-2 as a sweep: `--sweep` puts the lines of `sandbox/judge_draw_sweep.json` one at a time in front of a working mesh's draw. 50 lines: 35 as the original's, 12 differ in the layer (each recorded with why: slots of other kinds refuse the draw, `copy`, `unless_null`, slot to slot, `ResourceX = ref vb0`, `o0 = null`), 3 are not judged by this scene (answered by the character Core's extension, which the scene has not). The step fails on a regression and on a recorded difference that now agrees.
- Two differences the sweep found are fixed (both built on the original's source):
  - BUG-086: a matched section's post list ran although the pre list had bound an index buffer of its own. The original checks again in the post phase (CheckTextureOverrideCommand::run) against the buffer bound by then; a Mod's own has no hash and meets no section. `execute_matches` now does the same. No installed Mod writes `post` in a TextureOverride (209 `post` lines, all in Present, Key and ShaderRegex sections).
  - BUG-087: `ResourceX = ref ps-tN` took the application's texture although the lists had bound the slot; a list that saved its binding and put it back drew with the game's texture. It holds what the lists bound there (`pixel_held`).
- BUG-088 (OPEN), D-159, found by the whole suite: the EFMITTL the user installed on 2026-11-10 13:15 does not work in the layer on any build (the binaries of 2026-10-27 fail the same replay step). Its lists ask the pixel shader's declaration of every slot of a range, then the format and size of the game's texture at each. Fixed, from the original's source and the paired catalogue: a slot below t60 without a SPIR-V register is not declared (9091 of 9094 such slots agree with DX11), and a condition that asks a buffer of 32-bit words goes on when it is the same for both DX11 answers. Open: `ps-t[N]->Format`, `->Width`, `->Height`, `->Mips` of the game's texture are not answered, so the library's draws are still refused (224 plans in the replay, no translucency layer). The suite step "captured frame with a library call" is red for that reason.
- P3-3: every Mod's plans in every state as one number (`digest` of `--plan-folder-states`); scripts/check_mod_states.py compares it with the number accepted for the same files (build/plan-digests/accepted.json, this machine's) and fails when a Mod's plans change while its files did not, until `--accept`. Refusal sites in frontend/xxmi/draw_plan.cpp: 136 (`refuse(`), the number the review asks to see go down.
- P3-4, the list: what the offline state check leaves out and where each is checked instead (docs/ini-semantics.md). Its other half (hashed textures in the state check) is open.
- P4-1: ARCHITECTURE.md rewritten for 2.1 (the old one in archive/), CURRENT_STATE.md and TASKS.md shortened (history in archive/), PROJECT_OVERVIEW.md refreshed, docs/ini-semantics.md with the failure units and the matched-draw judge.

- Verified this round, sandbox: the whole suite (sandbox/run_all.py --no-build) on the final build: 54 steps passed, 1 failed, "captured frame with a library call", which is BUG-088 (the installed EFMITTL of 2026-11-10; red on the binaries of 2026-10-27 as well). Among the 54: live INI rendering 118 checks (the four host checks failing on the build before), the owned runtime's resource_gpu 11291 checks with validation clean, the INI runtime 500, the matrix 0 failures, the judge and its two draw steps, every section of 19 installed Mods planning in every state (6834 states), the other replays and the Vulkan validation steps. ## Every enabled INI an input (2026-10-30, D-155, docs/ini-semantics.md)

- The user: the parsing must take every INI under Mods as the original does, and handle a parse error the original's way. Now the folder generation's inputs are every enabled INI (except the native libraries' files and the folders of left-out character Mods): a plain Mod's Present and keys run, its settings persist, its texture-hash sections act in replaced draws; LIMIT-046 lifted. A declaration reference the layer does not take (`copy_desc`) leaves the destination null with the original-style warning once instead of refusing the generation or the draw.
- Failing first: the live INI scene's `plain` Mod (Present and a texture-hash section on the fixture's draw) and `bad` INI (a `copy_desc` in its post-Constants): 112/115 on the build before.
- Found on the way: the fixture's `run = CommandListUnbindAllRenderTargets` in its Present (`o0 = null`, no binding destination in the plan) ended the whole Present at that command every frame, so every Mod's Present lines parsed after it never ran (the plain Mod's `$presents` stayed 0, the settings were never saved). The Present and [Constants] lists now run each command on their own as the original does: a refused one does nothing with its warning once (DrawPlanAdapter::tolerate_refusals); a replaced draw's lists keep their all-or-nothing plan. With the owned GPU runtime a `copy_desc` of a file-backed buffer is made as owned work (the live scene's `bad` INI loads and presents without a warning), so the user's `\u6c64\u6c64` Mod, refused for that line only in the offline dry run, may already load in the game; offline the plan leaves such a copy null with a warning. The dry runs leave out refusals of services their stand-in lacks (the owned GPU domain, instance windows, render targets: Si Lunabelle and the new Mod offline) and a Present whose draws have no executor.
- Verification (sandbox): text, unit 117, original frontend host (Core pools 23/23 with a copy that leaves its destination null), INI runtime, the installed Mods' offline plans (20 of 20 loading; the folder generation, every key state, broken files), the Mods-folder and texture-filter replays, the judge, character replacement 377/377, live INI, owned Present 66/66. Red before as after: the library-call replay (BUG-078). Not deployed.

## INI parsing review (2026-10-30, D-154, docs/ini-semantics.md "How the files are read")

- The user asked for a review of the INI parsing. The parser is the original's; the layer around it diverged in: how a file is read (its own UTF-8 decoder refused a file with a byte sequence that is not UTF-8 whole, where the original reads up to that line: judge case `encoding`, `$before` 1, `$after` 0), how the Mods folder is walked (ordinal sort and a case-insensitive `.ini` against the original's case-insensitive sort and exact `.ini`; a `mod.INI` made the whole folder refuse), the installation's d3dx.ini never read (its [Rendering] keys, a command list it declares: LIMIT-039, a [Constants]/[Present]/[Key] written there), budgets of 128 and 64 INI files, and a Mod alone named by its ASCII file name instead of the original's implicit namespace (the user's new Mod `\u6c64\u6c64`, 2026-10-29, could not be tried alone). Behind the last: the native Core bridge widened a Mod's UTF-8 name byte by byte, so a Mod named in another script never found its own skeleton list; its lookups of a Mod's declarations now name what is missing.
- Failing first: runtime_test's Mods folder scene (a `Zeta` Mod's [Present] order, a `mod.INI`, a d3dx.ini beside Mods with a list the fixture runs, a character Mod in a folder named with a full-width colon without a namespace line): the whole folder refused on the build before; the judge's `encoding` case (4 values as the original's).
- The new Mod's real reason, shown now: `[Constants] resource... = copy_desc ...` in its post-Constants ("Unsupported declaration reference"), the construct that left Typhoeus out (D-131 step 1); the every-key-state check reports it NOT LOADED until copy_desc declaration references are taken (TASKS).
- Found on the way, pre-existing: BUG-078 (the vk01 library-call replay captures no translucency; HEAD built in a worktree of its own fails identically; translucency.ini changed 2026-10-29 22:52). The runtime test read the status JSON (UTF-8) with the console code page, so a package named in another script never compared equal; it reads UTF-8 now.
- Verification (sandbox): text, unit 117, original frontend host, INI runtime 499, the installed Mods' offline plans (dry run 19 of 20 loading, the folder generation, broken files), the Mods-folder and texture-filter replays, the judge (ten cases, `encoding` among them), character replacement 377/377, live INI 111/111, owned Present 66/66. Red, both before this change: the every-key-state check on the new Mod's copy_desc, and the library-call replay (BUG-078). Not deployed, not run in the game.

A buffer's by description, a 1D or 3D texture's, and keys that change the game's resources at creation, map or copy are inert, the rest of the Mod loads, and each is reported for its Mod (CharacterDiscovery::gaps: log, the first three as notices; check_mod_states prints them). What they found, fixed: a slot range of typed buffers binds nothing in the original (a view D3D11 refuses), where the layer refused; every view D3D11 does not make is now a null view; a resource's first reference adds its destination's bind flags (CustomResource::Substantiate); a texel-buffer view at an element the 16-byte minimum does not align (a Region) uses Vulkan 1.3's single-texel alignment.

## 2.1.1: the loader behind a proxy DLL, character meshes without resizable BAR (2026-10-28, BUG-074, BUG-075)

- The user asked for a review of the whole project after an AMD player's 2.0.9 log (the layer loaded, "no free memory near vulkan-1.dll for 0 stubs; nothing redirected", nothing after: no Mod). Reviewed for what breaks on other machines: injection and the loader (BUG-074), device creation (every added extension, feature and the upload queue already checked against support), memory types and heaps (BUG-075; the snapshot arenas, bone rings and translucency targets choose correctly), the layer's own shaders (bone_merge.hlsl, no subgroup operations), paths outside ASCII (wide or u8path everywhere, BUG-046 and LIMIT-042 earlier), compiler loading (full paths), formats (LIMIT-045), device teardown, the release check.
- BUG-074: the first module named vulkan-1.dll was taken for the loader. 2.1.1 patches only a module that exports vkGetInstanceProcAddr as code; a proxy's forwarders name the loader that is waited for (by full path, or a renamed copy by its file name); every module of that name is described in the log (path, exported names, where vkGetInstanceProcAddr forwards); a second real loader is logged and left alone. Test (modes): the sandbox copied next to a generated proxy vulkan-1.dll (every export of the system loader forwarded by full path; and one forwarding to a copy named vulkan-1-real.dll). Failing on 2.1.0 with the AMD log's exact line ("0 stubs; nothing redirected", no device), 50/50 on 2.1.1.
- BUG-075: character meshes went into host-visible device memory whatever its heap. This machine has resizable BAR (that type on the 7.71 GiB heap); without it the window is a heap of about 256 MiB the game uses too (four characters were 110 MB here). 2.1.1 maps device memory only when it is on the largest device-local heap (resizable BAR, integrated GPUs), otherwise copies the mesh into device memory on the layer's upload queue (staging in system memory, fence, then published), as D3D11 fills a DEFAULT buffer from its initial data (CustomResource::SubstantiateBuffer); host memory without an upload queue. Status `mesh_memory` (mapped, copied, host). Memory type choices skip protected, lazily allocated and AMD device-coherent types. Test (character replacement): the split-queue runs with `EFMI_VK_TEST_SMALL_BAR=1` (the test's child process: host-visible device memory taken for a heap of its own of 256 MiB) must copy, the others map; failing on the build before (16 checks), 377/377 with pixels and sync validation; the vk03 Mods-folder replay with the simulation passed (a 23.7 MB mesh copied in 8.1 ms, mapped 5.3-5.9 ms).
- check_release.py chose its layout Mod by a fixed name the user has since removed; it now takes the smallest character Mod the installed folder loads and every enabled folder that is not one.
- Evidence (sandbox): modes 50/50, character replacement 377/377, text, unit, probe end to end, Vulkan validation, the vk03 Mods-folder replay (small window simulated): all passed. Release check on EFMI-Vulkan-2.1.1.zip (SHA-256 dba416f31195a16396690b41b8fa8257ad4e29053ea09c9297d46c93f72bd391): passed (installed Mods 18 character Mods, none left out; both unpack layouts). Not run in the game; not on an AMD GPU (none here). 2.1.0 was never published; 2.1.1 supersedes it.

## 2.1.0: the release finds the EFMI folder from where it is unpacked (2026-10-27, BUG-073)

- The user downloaded v2.0.9 and unpacked it into EFMI\Mods: no Mod loaded (`../Mods` named EFMI\Mods\Mods). Moved to the EFMI folder it worked in the game (the user, 2026-10-27): the first game run of the release build.
- 2.1.0: `m2_mods = auto` (the release's): the first folder upward from the DLL's that holds Mods and Core\EFMI; every scan of the Mods folder leaves out the layer's own folder (its settings file is an INI); a first-chance fault outside the layer's folder is logged as `exception:` with a note, not as `crash:` (the game raises and handles one at every start; the 2.0.9 log showed it as a crash). The sandbox's `--handled-fault` raises one after its device exists; symbolize_crash.py reads both kinds of lines.
- Evidence (sandbox): check_release.py extended with the unpack layouts (an EFMI folder of its own with the installed Core, one character Mod and the libraries; the release in EFMI and in EFMI\Mods; an INI planted in its settings; the handled fault): failing on the 2.0.9 archive (the Mods folder layout found no Mod, the fault logged as a crash), passing on 2.1.0 (and the installed Mods: 18 character Mods, none left out); run_all's touched areas (20 steps: text, unit, both offline plan steps, every captured-frame replay, character replacement, live INI, probe end to end, modes, validation): all passed. Archive EFMI-Vulkan-2.1.0.zip, SHA-256 0e50731e675fe6ca3cae55e063fd907cf4673af284fbcacba703ee214f078ec8.
